- **ontology-conceptualizer**: Builds a conceptual model from requirements and reuse findings: glossaries, taxonomies / class hierarchies, property designs, module and layer design, and a BFO upper ontology category decision (continuant vs occurrent; quality vs role vs disposition). Covers all multi-pod env vars, the custom-model-icon perf history (base64-in-/api/models, fixed late 2025–Apr 2026), the official helm chart's gaps (bundled Redis is unsuitable for production; no HPA/PDB/probes/sticky sessions), and the catalog of known multi-pod issues with current status.
